Run it on the PC or the x86 mini with two build dirs. It covers the data stores the `upgrade` selftest stage opens (settings, dashboard sources, inbox); approvals and an installed app are not created by that stage yet.
* KI-751 **F-CORE-18: events are still pulled (P-B, 2026-11-13).** Only the base64 chunk encoding of contract 0.2 is built. A pushed event channel needs a streaming response in the developer-mode listener (it sends one buffered body per request today); until then a client long-polls with wait_ms (at most 10 s).
* KI-780 **F-CORE-24: wizards can be driven by chat but there is no screen and no migration yet (P-B, 2026-11-13).** The chat tools and the install hook are built and tested with fakes. No tile draws a wizard, the real environment has no migration runner (a migration step fails with a plain sentence and touches nothing), and the install hook was tested through its waiting-list functions, not by installing a real package with a wizards list.
* KI-781 **F-LINAPP-16: the root-to-image conversion has no way to be called yet (P-B, 2026-11-13).** ocipush_root.rs works on a tar in memory and is tested on hand-made archives; no `linux` command, chat tool or Linux apps page turns a sandbox into an image, it reads an uncompressed tar only (the .tar.gz of the sandbox archive must be gunzipped first), and no image made this way was started on a cluster.
* KI-744 **The reranking request was not tried against a real Lemonade server (F-RAG-14).** The shape (`POST <base>/reranking` with model, query and documents; results with index and relevance_score) follows the Jina-style API; a differing server answer reads as "skipped" and the search order stands. The Documents page of Settings has the order control (key O, M) for administrators.
* KI-745 **Graph RAG finds names by capital letters (F-RAG-15).** Lower-case names, scripts without capitals and names that start a sentence alone are missed; there is no model-based extraction, the Settings Documents page only switches it (key G), and the graph lives in memory only (rebuilt when a collection changes).
* KI-746 **Only single web pages are kept as documents (F-RAG-16).** No crawling and no refresh by itself: a page is replaced when it is kept again (chat rag_add_page, or key M in the browser).
* KI-747 **Text from fonts without a Unicode map is read only for embedded Identity-H TrueType fonts (F-PDF-08).** Type 1, CFF and legacy encodings with a private glyph order are still not read.
* KI-748 **DDC/CI cannot reach a monitor yet (F-KBD-08).** The messages and the rules are written; opening `/dev/i2c-N` needs `i2c-dev` in the kernel and a monitor that supports DDC/CI; there is no Settings choice of monitor and no chat tool.
* KI-770 **Camera alert descriptions were not tried with a real vision model (F-CAM-18).** Only a fake model answered; the description follows the plain notice as a second inbox item.
* KI-771 **Phone and headset keys were not tried on a real phone (F-NET-27).** Next and previous step the shared play queue since 2026-11-14; they were tried with fake packets only.

* KI-790 **Tiles show pictures only through the web service, and no tile uses one yet (F-WASM-12, 2026-11-13, narrowed Q-D 2026-11-14).** The picture table and both renderers agree in a real browser, and the web service fills the table from a tile definition and sends the pictures a frame names. * KI-749 **Theme files are chosen by chat tool and apply at the next sign-in (F-SHELL-62).** The theme card lists the files and applies a choice at once since 2026-11-13, checked in tests and not on a screen; an app that draws with colours outside the shared palette follows the base theme's lightness mapping, which a file cannot change.
* KI-772 **Moving browser data between machines does not take the key (F-VAULT-11).** The sealed file moves by chat tool; the browser key must already be in the vault on the other machine. * KI-930 **F-LINAPP-14: the pod fallback never ran on a real cluster (P-C, 2026-11-13).** Root to image to pod is tested against a fake kubectl and a fake importer. Whether containerd accepts the OCI layout from `k3s ctr images import`, and whether the pod then runs, is unproven. A Podman-backed dev cluster is refused.
* KI-931 **F-LINAPP-14: the pod only sleeps (P-C, 2026-11-13).** The container command is `sleep infinity` (the person uses `kubectl exec`), so a root without a `sleep` program never reaches Running and is removed again. The image has no entry point of the app.
* KI-932 **F-LINAPP-12: several windows per tile never ran with real X servers (P-C, 2026-11-13).** Slots give each window its own display number, socket and authority file, and the slot logic of the script is run in sh in a test, but two Xvnc servers in one sandbox were not started. A sandbox installed before slots gets its script replaced on the first second window.
* KI-933 **F-LINAPP-12: the Alt mapping is partial (P-C, 2026-11-13).** Alt plus a typed character reaches the program as Alt while a window runs. Alt with the arrows, Alt+Tab and Alt alone are not sent, and on the Mac profile the Option key may already be taken by the profile before it reaches the tile.
* KI-990 **Edit tile: the toolbar is cut at the right edge in a phone-sized window (2026-11-14).** In the web UI at 390 px the tile is 332 px wide and the "CLEAR" button is clipped; the toolbar is laid out for a wider area. Found by the app isolation check; not fixed.
* KI-991 **Web tiles: typing from a phone's soft keyboard is untried (2026-11-14).** The tiles draw on a canvas and the page forwards keys; whether the soft keyboard appears and its text reaches the Edit and Chat tiles on a real phone was not checked (the check only renders the tiles).
* KI-960 **F-PDF-08: only Type 1 programs with a plain-text encoding array are read (Q-C, 2026-11-14).** A Type 1 program's built-in encoding is read from its head; CFF (`FontFile3`) and Type 1 programs whose glyph order is private are still not read. No real-world PDF corpus was tried.
* KI-961 **F-PICT-01: the picture density is the reader's page scale, never seen on a dense screen (Q-C, 2026-11-14).** The choice follows `srcset` and `min-resolution` in tests only; script `devicePixelRatio` follows it since 2026-11-14.
* KI-962 **F-RAG-16: a kept page is refreshed only when it is kept again (Q-C, 2026-11-14).** No schedule, no crawling and no browser button.
* KI-963 **Test raises of 2026-11-14 (Q-C) are on fakes, loopback and a Mac (Apple silicon).** Bluetooth, sound and Setup rows were not tried on the real machines they name; three macOS-only tests fail on this Mac (the loopback address test of devctl, the real mDNSResponder test of netscan, the pipe size test of webmix).
* KI-980 **F-RAG-15: a sentence-first name counts only when it stands alone with a capital elsewhere (Q-C, 2026-11-14).** A name that never appears inside a sentence is still taken for a capital letter, and the other limits of KI-745 stay.
* KI-981 **F-QA-05, F-SHARE-01: the second-reader tests need tools on the machine (Q-C, 2026-11-14).** The cpio, hdiutil and bsdtar tests and the curl test skip with a message where the tool is missing; on this Mac all ran. A FAT volume made by a real tool and an image of IYAKKI were not scanned.
* KI-982 **F-NET-27: next and previous step the queue (Q-C, 2026-11-14), not tried with a phone.** Done in the shared player. A step starts one track at a time, so a queue the chat handed to an outside player as one playlist file stops being one playlist after a step.

* KI-940 **The guarded Os has no on-screen card yet (F-ARCH-05/06, F-CORE-13, Q-A 2026-11-14).** Apps get `cx.os` as a `GuardedOs` that asks the broker for every call and logs it, but the per-use card for the hard ask (`system.memory.set`) is the person's own key or click bound to the event being handled (a call inside that dispatch, or by a job that took the one-second single-use ticket there; anything else is `no_card`), not a card that names the app with Allow and Deny buttons. Only the Memory and the hardware pages of Settings go through it; the Bluetooth page, the wifi, storage and other pages still hold their own handles (the ratchet list). Tested on the fake machine and through a real file log, never on a screen.
* KI-970 **`iyakki-usb --update` never wrote a real stick (F-UPG-05, Q-D 2026-11-14).** Tested on made-up FAT32 volumes only, and `fsck.vfat` was not on the test host. It does not check the signature of `update.json`, cannot create new files with long names, and needs the drive unmounted (a mounted stick is the kernel's, not ours).

* KI-971 **One reading-aloud test is flaky when the voice modules run together (F-VOICE-05, Q-D 2026-11-14).** `hostengines::tests::chat_replies_are_read_with_the_engine_found_at_the_speed_of_the_setting` fails about one run in two with the other voice tests (the fake engine's input file does not appear within six seconds; cause not found); alone it passes. The row stays at Test 30 until it is stable.
* KI-941 **The domain listener's HTTP/2 was tried only on loopback with curl and a hand-written client (F-CLOUD-11, Q-A 2026-11-14).** The listener serves HTTP/2 (prior knowledge on a plain port, ALPN `h2` on a TLS port) by running each stream through its HTTP/1.1 code. Real curl (nghttp2) got the same answers as over HTTP/1.1, including a 200 000-byte upload and a 3 MB download that needs window updates, over plain and TLS; the flow-control window, floods and malformed frames are tested with a small client. A headless Chrome loaded a page and its twelve parallel requests over TLS and ALPN h2 (the test skips where there is no Chrome); no browser was used by a person, nothing ran on a machine with real names and ports 80 and 443, server push, priorities and trailers are not served, and since ALPN now offers `h2` first every TLS route on the listener meets HTTP/2 clients: `Options::http2 = false` turns it off.
* KI-971 **Two reading-aloud tests raced on the fake engine's files (F-VOICE-05, fixed Q-D 2026-11-14).** The fake engines write their input first and their arguments or pid after it, and the tests stopped them in between; they now wait for both. 40 runs of the voice modules in parallel on the Mac passed. Other timing tests of the mixer (`mixer::stress_tests::the_heard_position_is_held_back...`, `mixer::tests::a_live_source_makes_the_mixer_reopen...`) still fail about one run in ten under load; they are the mixer's own and are not changed here.
* KI-941 **The domain listener's HTTP/2 was tried only on loopback with curl and a hand-written client (F-CLOUD-11, Q-A 2026-11-14).** The listener serves HTTP/2 (prior knowledge on a plain port, ALPN `h2` on a TLS port) by running each stream through its HTTP/1.1 code. Real curl (nghttp2) got the same answers as over HTTP/1.1, including a 200 000-byte upload and a 3 MB download that needs window updates, over plain and TLS; the flow-control window, floods and malformed frames are tested with a small client. No browser tried it, nothing ran on a machine with real names and ports 80 and 443, server push, priorities and trailers are not served, and since ALPN now offers `h2` first every TLS route on the listener meets HTTP/2 clients: `Options::http2 = false` turns it off.
* KI-942 **The setup wizard screen was never seen on a real screen (F-CORE-24, Q-A 2026-11-14).** `w` on Settings, System update opens the questions as forms, the checks and moves as a card and the summary, on the same run as the assistant's tools. It is tested on a fake machine with the keyboard and drawn in tests only; no installed app brings a wizard whose migration does anything yet (KI-780), and a cancelled setup only stays on the waiting list, it is not offered again anywhere but this page.
* KI-971 **Two reading-aloud tests raced on the fake engine's files (F-VOICE-05, fixed Q-D 2026-11-14).** The fake engines write their input first and their arguments or pid after it, and the tests stopped them in between; they now wait for both. 40 runs of the voice modules in parallel on the Mac passed. Other timing tests of the mixer (`mixer::stress_tests::the_heard_position_is_held_back...`, `mixer::tests::a_live_source_makes_the_mixer_reopen...`) still fail about one run in ten under load; they are the mixer's own and are not changed here.
* KI-983 **F-PDF-04, F-QA-14: the second-reader tests need sips and shasum (Q-C, 2026-11-14).** They skip with a message where the tool is missing; only the first page and the page size of a written PDF were compared.
* KI-943 **The kept bundle members have a reader, but no tile calls it yet (F-CORE-26, Q-A 2026-11-14).** `store::bundle::kept_members(store, kind)` hands a tile the verified JSON objects of the agent, template, theme and extension members of the installed bundles. The agent picker, the template list, the theme chooser and the extension list do not ask it: they have no list of bundle members yet, and what a theme or an agent preset must contain is not fixed in a schema.
* KI-984 **MCP, MEDIA and RADIO tiles name agent data types (Q-C, 2026-11-14).** The three migrated tiles no longer name the services or the shell context, but `ServerSpec`, `Tool`, `MediaStore`, `MediaIndex`, `DownloadQueue`, `Station`, `RadioState` and the language lists still come from `iyakki-agent`, so they cannot move into a crate that depends on `iyakki-uikit` and `iyakki-tile-api` only until those types move (docs/development/APP-MIGRATION-INVENTORY.md, column agent paths).
